([marwen-abid/dot-env](https://github.com/marwen-abid/dot-env/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cloud-compat))

- **cloud-compile-phone-install**: Autonomously offloads heavy Android Gradle builds and JVM compilation to GitHub Codespaces (16 GB RAM Azure VM), retrieves the generated APK with zero local memory consumption, and deploys it directly to a physical USB-connected phone via ADB. Trigger on /cloud-install, /build-phone, or whenever user asks to compile, build, test, or install the app on their mobile device. ([meabhisheksoni/skill](https://github.com/meabhisheksoni/skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cloud-compile-phone-install) · ★1)
- **cloud-compile-phone-install**: Autonomously offloads heavy Android Gradle builds and JVM compilation to GitHub Codespaces (16 GB RAM Azure VM), retrieves the generated APK with zero local memory consumption, and deploys it directly to a physical USB-connected phone via ADB. Features full autonomous self-healing: if the Codespace is deleted or expires, it automatically re-provisions a fresh 16GB cloud VM, bootstraps Java 17 & Android SDK 36, and completes the build with zero human intervention. Trigger on /cloud-install, /build-phone, or whenever user asks to compile, build, test, or install the app on their mobile device. ([meabhisheksoni/skill](https://github.com/meabhisheksoni/skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cloud-compile-phone-install/cloud-compile-phone-install) · ★1)
